- **cofounder-check**: Use this skill when the user asks to validate their repo against CoFounder guardrails — "run cofounder check", "verify the changes are safe", "is this PR-ready?", "check for guardrail violations". Also use when the user mentions 'coglog', 'cog-log', 'coglog', 'meta-log', 'self-narrative', 'current_focus', 'turn memory', or asks the AI to reflect on its own reasoning process across turns. ([HarmoniaEpic/CogLog](https://github.com/HarmoniaEpic/CogLog/tree/HEAD/coglog-skill/coglog))
- **coglog-viz**: Provide cognitive continuity across conversation turns with per-turn visualization. Use this skill whenever maintaining context between turns matters and you want a visual artifact of each turn's cognitive state. Includes all CogLog functionality (read/write/clear) plus automatic React viewer generation on every write. Also use when the user mentions 'coglog', 'cog-log', 'coglog-viz', 'metalog', 'meta-log', 'self-narrative', 'current_focus', 'turn memory', or asks the AI to reflect on its own reasoning process across turns. ([HarmoniaEpic/CogLog](https://github.com/HarmoniaEpic/CogLog/tree/HEAD/coglog-skill/coglog-viz))

- **cognitive-load-theory**: Sweller's Cognitive Load Theory (CLT) for agents writing skill content, designing prompts, building UI/dashboards, and authoring documentation. - **cognitive-path-comic-content**: 将法律、AI工具、教育、职场、商业、健康等复杂知识转化为适合小红书、抖音图文、公众号和视频号发布的连续漫画图文。凡用户提出“以X为主题画漫画”“围绕X做漫画图文”“做一组科普漫画”“把案例或文章改成漫画”“一页纸漫画”“漫画讲解”“首图加正文图”“做5到8张连续图文”“用分镜解释这个问题”“把专业内容做成可收藏图片”，即使没有说出Skill名称，也应使用本 Skill。它以用户认知路径为核心，动态规划5—8页、每页1—2格，完成事实查证、选题建模、角色连续性、逐页提示词、成图质检和发布文案；不是单纯生成漂亮插画。 ([xiaogege6697/cognitive-path-comic-content](https://github.com/xiaogege6697/cognitive-path-comic-content/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)

- **cognitive-profile**: 在对话里慢慢形成一份可修正的用户画像。用于用户要求建立、查看、更新画像， 希望回答更贴合自己，或反复纠正"不像我""太官方""理解错了"的场景。 也用于用户说"记住我的偏好""以后默认这样""按我之前习惯""别再这样" "不要记录""删掉画像""你记了什么""你忘了"，或普通任务里需要沿用已知偏好的场景。 "记住"指向的是长期偏好时才触发；"记住这个端口号"这类任务内指令不算。 只记录会影响后续回答的偏好、判断习惯、表达边界和待确认的地方。 ([TashanGKD/tashan-research-skills](https://github.com/TashanGKD/tashan-research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-profile) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **cognitive-profile**: Creates, reads and reassesses the programmer's cognitive learning profile. - **cognitive-quadrant-general**: Use this cognitive reflection workflow to clarify complex issues, inspect assumptions and blind spots, compare decisions, iterate plans or ideas, expand knowledge boundaries, and create verifiable next actions. Trigger on requests such as “help me understand this better,” “what am I missing,” “analyze this choice,” “expand my perspective,” “iterate this idea,” “帮我看清这个问题”, “检查认知盲区”, “分析一个选择”, or “拓宽知识边界”. Do not use for simple translation, formatting, or a single stable fact. - **cognitive-reappraisal**: 认知重评提示语原文，Beck 的想法记录。用户被一句话或一条评论困住、内耗、反复觉得自己不行、想太多、自动冒出负面念头时使用。触发词包括 认知重评、想法记录、CBT、内耗、我是不是不行、被一句话伤到、想太多、自动想法、思维偏差、情绪上来了、对自己太苛刻。一次只问一个问题，产出一句更平衡的替代想法和一个很小的动作。别人的说法要查真假，用 fact-check。提到自伤或已经严重到做不了记录，停下来建议专业帮助，这不是治疗。 ([LearnPrompt/laoyeye](https://github.com/LearnPrompt/laoyeye/tree/HEAD/skills/solving/cognitive-reappraisal) · ★110)

- **cognitive-reorganize**: 从整个工作区出发，系统性地重组认知结构并整理全局文档归属。适用于首次建立认知结构、或积累了大量散落文档需要批量归档时。基于三大闭环框架（Loop1 Skill体系 / Loop2 思维体系 / Loop3 场景投射）做全工作区分类路由。触发词：「系统整理」「重组认知结构」「从文档构建认知结构」「整合文档」「全量梳理」「认知结构乱了」「全工作区整理」「文档归属梳理」「如何加入认知结构」「怎么加入认知结构」「按规范加入」「未更新的文档怎么处理」「这些文档要怎么整理」「有没有关于X的已有文档」。注意：若用户仅询问流程而非执行重组，执行 Step 0.5（检查分类清单现状）后，先向用户展示已有文档状态和分类建议，再确认是否执行完整重组。 ([TashanGKD/tashan-cursor-skills](https://github.com/TashanGKD/tashan-cursor-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-reorganize) · ★25 · 3 copies)
